def project_feasible(y, n):
  y=np.clip(y,0,1)
  S=y.sum(); target=n/2
  if abs(S-target)<1e-14 return y
  if S<target:
    mask = y<1-1e-12
    room=(1-y)[mask]; tot=room.sum(); if tot==0 fail
    y[mask]+= diff/tot*room; but might exceed? diff/tot≤1? If diff ≤ tot, factor≤1. Since y clipped to <1 for mask, y+factor room≤1. One step exact? factor = diff/tot, room sum=tot, so sum increases by diff. Values at boundary y=1 excluded; y<1 might reach 1 if factor=1; okay. If some y=1 not mask. If some y=0 but room=1. This exact if no numerical. If S>target similar. This one-step works because linear scaling along room from current to max; if sum deficit <= room total. Good. But if S<target and some y close to 1 not included. It yields sum target. Need clip for numerical. So O(n). Previous loop unnecessary. However if target within? yes. If S<target but room total < diff? impossible since room total = n - S if clip? Actually only y<1; room total = ∑(1-y) over non-ones = n-S if no ones? If ones, total room = n-S, diff=target-S≤n-S. If ones excluded but room total = n-S (since ones room 0). So ok. If S>target, tot=S, diff=target-S negative magnitude≤S. Scale non-zeros. Good. So one-step exact. Use eps to avoid zero.
But during L-BFGS with penalty, projection after; for projected gradient each step can use one-step projection.

def _fft_plan(n):
  key=n
  if key in cache: return
  Nc = 1
  while Nc < 2*n-1: Nc <<=1
  Nw = 1
  while Nw < 3*n-2: Nw <<=1
  cache[key]=(Nc,Nw)

  h = project_feasible
  best_c=exact_c5
  n=len
  for iter while time:

    w=np.zeros(2n-1); w[active]=1/len(active)

    g = grad - grad.mean()

    if gnorm <1e-12: break
    direction = -g

    if no break: shrink? maybe break or try next alpha.

import numpy as np, time
def exact_c5(h): ...
def project_feasible(y): ...
def linear_h(n): ...
def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
  t0=time.time(); deadline=t0+budget_s-5
  best_h=None; best_c=inf
  # parent
  try:
    hp=np.array(initial_h_values, dtype=float).copy()
    hp=project_feasible(hp)
    c=exact_c5(hp); update
  except: pass
  # linear for candidate n powers of two up to 2048
  for n in [8,16,32,64,128,256,512,1024,2048]:
    if time.time()>deadline: break
    h=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/n
    # ensure exact sum? maybe if abs(sum-target)>1e-12: project

    c=exact_c5(h); update
    # decreasing maybe same by reversal? np.correlate symmetric? Reversal gives same max? Probably. No need.
  # maybe evaluate triangular? Could include but not needed.
  # If time remains and best_c > 0.34? (not) run local? We can return early.
  return best_h, best_c, len(best_h)

Now design local methods in detail.

Fast c and gradient functions as earlier. Need implement robust FFT gradient. This is useful for active/projected.

Let's derive S0/Splus/Sminus with Wpos mapping for our c_d weights. Our w indices correspond d order idx=0..M-1 where d=-n+1+idx. We built Wpos array in that order. Derivations for Splus/Sminus assumed w_d with d actual. Good. Let's test with small n=2 earlier using Wpos [w_-1,w0,w1] and Splus formula gave correct. So fast_grad okay.

But we must be careful with FFT correlation `corr = irfft(Fh * conj(FW), N=Nw)`. Fh and FW are zero-padded sequences with h at indices 0..n-1, W at indices 0..M-1. The circular correlation `corr[s] = Σ_i h_i W_{(i-s) mod N}`. We need Splus[j] = Σ_i h_i W_{i-j+n-1} = Σ_i h_i W_{i-(j-n+1)}. So s = j-n+1 mod N. For j=0..n-1, s = N-n+1...N-1,0. We set Splus[0:n-1]=corr[N-n+1:Nw], Splus[-1]=corr[0]. Good.
Sminus via conv = ifft(Fh*FW): conv[t]=Σ_i h_i W_{t-i mod N}; need t=j+n-1. Sminus[j]=conv[j+n-1] for j=0..n-1 (indices n-1..2n-2). Good.
S0 prefix: lo=n-1-j, hi=2n-1-j (exclusive) as array. For j=0: w indices n-1..2n-2 corresponding d=0..n-1. Correct (valid d for i=0: 0..n-1). j=n-1: 0..n-2 d=-n+1..0. Good.
Thus grad = S0 - Splus - Sminus (in c units). Test n=2 values earlier works.

Fast c via autocorr: Need Q_d = ∑ h_i h_{i+d}. Our autocorr r from ifft(A*conj(A)): r[k]=Σ_i h_i h_{i-k mod N}. For d positive, Q_d = Σ h_i h_{i+d} = r[-d mod N]. For d negative dd, Q_d = r[dd]. So q_idx = (-d)%Nc. Good. L_d prefix: valid i: 0≤i<n and 0≤i+d<n -> i range max(0,-d) to min(n-1,-d+n-1). Sum h. For d≥0: i 0..n-1-d => P[n-d]. d<0: i -d..n-1 => P[n]-P[-d]. Good. c=L-Q.
Need check c values correspond to h_i(1-h_{i+d}) = sum_i h_i - sum h_i h_{i+d}; yes.

Let's test small n=2 with h=[0,1]. Our c_d:

```
def gradient_top(h, active):
 n=len; grad=np.zeros(n); dx=2/n
 for idx in active:
   k=idx-(n-1)
   if k>=0:
    nv=n-k
    grad[0:nv] += (1-h[k:n])*dx
    grad[k:n] += -h[0:nv]*dx
   else:
    kk=-k; nv=n-kk
    grad[kk:n] += (1-h[0:nv])*dx
    grad[0:nv] += -h[kk:n]*dx
 return grad
```

grad[0:nv] += (1-h[k:n]) (first factor)
grad[k:n] += -h[0:nv] (second)
For d<0: kk=-d; c_d=∑_{i=kk}^{n-1} h_i(1-h_{i-kk}). First: grad[kk:n] +=1-h[0:nv]; second: grad[0:nv]+= -h[kk:n]. Yes. Multiply dx. Sum over active.

```
_FAST_CACHE = {}
def _fast_plan(n):
   if n in _FAST_CACHE: return _FAST_CACHE[n]
   M=2*n-1
   Nc=1
   while Nc < 2*n-1: Nc <<=1
   d=np.arange(-n+1,n,dtype=int)
   q_idx=(-d)%Nc
   pos=d>=0
   neg=~pos
   # for S0 arrays maybe
   S0_lo=np.arange(n,0,-1)? We can compute in function.
   plan=(Nc,d,q_idx,pos,neg)
   _FAST_CACHE[n]=plan
   return plan
```
fast_c:
```
def fast_c(h):
 n=len(h); Nc,d,q_idx,pos,neg=_fast_plan(n)
 A=np.fft.rfft(h,N=Nc)
 r=np.fft.irfft(A*np.conj(A),N=Nc)
 Q=r[q_idx]
 P=np.empty(n+1); P[0]=0.0; np.cumsum(h,P[1:])
 total=P[n]
 L=np.empty(2*n-1)
 L[pos]=P[n-d[pos]]  # d pos ints
 L[neg]=total-P[-d[neg]]
 c=L-Q
 # c has tiny errors; maybe return c
 return c
```
Need d pos array length n? d[:?]. pos selects d>=0 (n values). Good.
For n=500, A rfft length513? Nc=1024? rfft size513. good.

But fast_c computes rfft A each call. If line search many calls, okay.

```
def fast_grad(h,w):
 n=len; Nc,Nw,d,q_idx,pos,neg,lo,hi = plan
 W = w  # length M
 Fh=np.fft.rfft(h,N=Nw)
 FW=np.fft.rfft(w,N=Nw)
 conv=np.fft.irfft(Fh*FW,N=Nw)
 corr=np.fft.irfft(Fh*np.conj(FW),N=Nw)
 Sminus=conv[n-1:2*n-1]  # length n? indices n-1 to 2n-2 inclusive -> slice n-1:2*n-1
 s_start=Nw-n+1
 Splus=np.empty(n)
 if n>1: Splus[:-1]=corr[s_start:Nw]
 Splus[-1]=corr[0]
 # S0 prefix
 wcum=np.empty(M+1); wcum[0]=0; np.cumsum(w,wcum[1:])
 S0=wcum[hi]-wcum[lo]
 return S0 - Splus - Sminus
```
Check slice lengths: n=500, 2*n-1=999; Sminus conv[499:999] length500. Splus corr[2048-500+1=1549:2048] length500? 2048-1549=499, plus Splus[-1]=corr[0] => total500. Good. If n=1, edge. n>=2.

def line_search_active(h, g, best_c, deadline):
    steps=[0.05,0.02,0.01,0.005,0.002,0.001,0.0005,0.0002,0.0001,5e-5,2e-5,1e-5]

       cf=fast_c5(h_try)
       if cf < best_c - 1e-13:
          ce=exact_c5(h_try)
          if ce < best_c - 1e-14:
             return h_try, ce, True
    return h,best_c,False

Active gradient function direct top:
```
def gradient_top_exact(h, active):
    n=len(h); dx=2.0/n; grad=np.zeros(n)
    for idx in active:
       k=idx-(n-1)
       if k>=0:
          nv=n-k
          wm=dx
          grad[0:nv] += wm*(1.0-h[k:n])
          grad[k:n] += -wm*h[0:nv]
       else:
          kk=-k; nv=n-kk; wm=dx
          grad[kk:n] += wm*(1.0-h[0:nv])
          grad[0:nv] += -wm*h[kk:n]
    # if active length K, average? We can divide by len(active) in caller.
    return grad
```
If average, divide by K. In projected pass, `g = gradient_top_exact(h, active) / len(active)` then subtract mean. Good.

def resample(h_old, n_new):
   n_old=len(h_old)
   x_old=np.linspace(0,2,n_old)
   x_new=np.linspace(0,2,n_new)
   h_new=np.interp(x_new,x_old,h_old)
   return project_feasible(h_new)
```

Configs for lbfgs_smoothed:
```
def lbfgs_smoothed(h, best_c, deadline, tag=''):
    n=len(h); target=n/2; bounds=[(0,1)]*n
    configs = [(50,1e-4,3000),(200,1e-3,4000),(1000,1e-3,5000),(5000,0.01,6000),(20000,0.05,8000),(100000,0.1,10000)]
    x=h.copy()
    for alpha,mu,maxfun in configs:
       if time.time()>deadline-3: break
       t_start=time.time(); allowed=maxfun depending n? 
       try:
          res=minimize(smooth_func_penalty, x, jac=smooth_grad_penalty, args=(n,alpha,mu), method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds, options={'maxiter':3000,'ftol':1e-16,'gtol':1e-13,'maxfun':maxfun,'maxls':30})
          x=project_feasible(res.x); c=exact_c5(x)
          if c<best_c: best_c=c; h=x
       except: pass
    return h,best_c
```
Need `smooth_func_penalty` and `smooth_grad_penalty` top-level. They call fast_c. Use `dx=2/n`, `cC=fast_c(x)*dx`.

Active projected refine: use exact line search or fast. Let's implement with fast line search and exact verify. Time check each outer.
```
def active_projected_refine(h, best_c, deadline, max_outer=200, ks=(3,5,10,20)):
   h=project_feasible; best_c=exact maybe passed
   for outer in range(max_outer):
      if time.time()>deadline: break
      c=fast_c(h); m=c.max()
      improved=False
      for K in ks:
         if time.time()>deadline: break
         active=np.argsort(-c)[:K]
         grad=gradient_top(h,active)/K
         g=grad-grad.mean()
         gnorm=np.linalg.norm(g)
         if gnorm<1e-12: continue
         step=0.05
         for _ in range(14):
            if time.time()>deadline: break
            h_try=project_feasible(h - step*g)
            cf=fast_c5(h_try)
            if cf < best_c - 1e-13:
                ce=exact_c5(h_try)
                if ce < best_c - 1e-14:
                    h=h_try; best_c=ce; improved=True; break
            step*=0.5
            if step<1e-7: break
         if improved: break
      # also try softmax weights with alpha 50, 200? maybe if top fails?
      if not improved:
         # try a very small random perturbation? maybe break to avoid loop
         break
   return h,best_c
```
If no improvement in first outer, break. Might stop too early. We can loop over K multiple times even if no? If no improvement for any K, no clear gradient; break. Good.
Could add `if not improved: # try active with all top 50` already. If still none, break.

But line search accepts only if fast_c5 < best_c -1e-13. If improvement tiny (<1e-13) not needed. Target diff 1e-5, fine.

def softmax_projected_refine(h,best_c,deadline, alphas=(10,50,200)):

      c=fast_c(h); cC=dx*c; w=softmax(cC,alpha)
      g=dx*fast_grad(h,w); g-=mean; line search

  return

Let's implement fast softmax in C units:
```
def c_to_weights(c, alpha):
    if len(c)==0: return zeros
    m = c.max()
    sh = alpha*(c-m)
    shm = sh.max()
    e = np.exp(sh-shm)
    s=e.sum()
    if s<=0: return np.full(len,1/len)
    return e/s
```
`c` can be counts; alpha count. For L-BFGS original no penalty, use alpha count? Let's choose alphas count [10,50,200,1000,5000]. Because c count range ~190; for near max differences count maybe 0.02, alpha 5000 gives weight concentration. Use continuation.

L-BFGS original penalty function:
```
def orig_smooth_obj(x,n,alpha,mu):
    cC = fast_c(x)*(2/n)
    val = smooth_value_cC(cC,alpha)
    diff=x.sum()-n/2
    return val + mu*diff*diff
def orig_smooth_grad(x,n,alpha,mu):
    cC=fast_c(x)*(2/n)
    w=weights_cC(cC,alpha)
    g=(2/n)*fast_grad(x,w)
    diff=x.sum()-n/2
    g = g + 2*mu*diff*np.ones(n)
    return g
```
If x slightly out of sum, okay. Bounds maintain.

Now, exact_c5 implementation:
```
def exact_c5(h):
    n=len(h)
    if n<=0: return float('inf')
    dx=2.0/n
    vals=np.correlate(h, 1.0-h, mode='full')
    return float(np.max(vals)*dx)
```
This matches task. For large n, use np.correlate direct. If h not feasible but in bounds, okay.

`project_feasible` robust:
```
def project_feasible(y):
    n=len(y)
    h=np.asarray(y,dtype=float).copy()
    np.clip(h,0.0,1.0,out=h)
    target=0.5*n
    diff=target-h.sum()
    if diff>1e-14:
       mask=h<1.0-1e-15
       room=(1.0-h)[mask]
       tot=room.sum()
       if tot>0:
          h[mask]+= (diff/tot)*room
       else: # all ones but diff>0 impossible unless target>n
          pass
    elif diff<-1e-14:
       mask=h>1e-15
       tot=h[mask].sum()
       if tot>0:
          h[mask]+= (diff/tot)*h  # diff negative
       else: pass
    np.clip(h,0.0,1.0,out=h)
    # final tiny correction
    diff=target-h.sum()
    if abs(diff)>0:
       mask=(h>1e-12)&(h<1-1e-12)
       if mask.any():
          idx=np.where(mask)[0][0]
          h[idx]+=diff
       else:
          idx=0; h[idx]+=diff
       np.clip(h,0,1,out=h)
    return h
```
When diff<0, `h[mask] += (diff/tot)*h` decreases proportionally. If some zeros excluded. If after scaling some negative due rounding clip. Final correction may break sum? Dot.
For symmetric y clip, use simple `np.clip`, no sum.

Implement symmetric L-BFGS configs:
```
def lbfgs_symmetric(h, best_c, deadline, phase_s=150):
    n=len(h); if n%2: resample? h sym resample to even.
    y = h[:n//2]
    n2=n//2
    bounds=[(0,1)]*n2
    configs=[(100,2000),(500,2500),(2000,3000),(10000,3500),(50000,4000)] maybe C units
    for alpha,maxfun in configs:
       if time.time()>deadline-phase_margin: break
       try:
         res=minimize(sym_smooth_obj, y, jac=sym_smooth_grad, args=(n,alpha), method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds, options={'maxiter':2000,'ftol':1e-15,'gtol':1e-12,'maxfun':maxfun,'maxls':30})
         y_new=res.x; h_new=sym_full(y_new,n); h_new=project_feasible(h_new)
         # maybe symmetrize again? project may break; do h_new=symmetrize_anti(h_new)
         h_new,s=consider...
         if improved: y=h_new[:n//2] (after sym)
       except: pass
    return h,best_c
```
Use `consider` which projects; but if y for next needs symmetric, call `h_sym=symmetrize_anti(h)` and set y=h_sym[:n2]. `consider` returns project, not necessarily sym. We can update best_h (feasible). For continuing, use `h_sym=symmetrize_anti(h)` and y. Good.

def active_symmetric_refine(h,best_c,deadline,phase_s):
 n=len(h)
 h=symmetrize_anti(h); if exact<best update
 y=h[:n//2]
 for outer...
   h=sym_full(y,n); c=fast_c(h); for K...
     gfull=grad_top(h,active)/K; gy=gfull[:n//2]-gfull[n-1::-1]
     # no mean; but maybe subtract? y changes sum exactly? h sum changes by 2? Let's check. y_i increase δ, h_i +δ, h_pair -δ, total sum unchanged exactly (1-y pair). Floating may drift. No need mean.
     for step:
       y_try=np.clip(y - step*gy,0,1)
       h_try=sym_full(y_try,n)
       ctry=exact_c5(h_try)
       if improve: h=project/sym; y=...; best=ctry; improved
```

`project_feasible`: Ensure output 1D.
```
def project_feasible(y):
    h=np.asarray(y,dtype=float).copy()
    if h.size==0: return h
    np.clip(h,0.0,1.0,out=h)
    target=0.5*len(h)
    s=h.sum()
    diff=target-s
    if diff>1e-14:
       mask=h < (1.0-1e-15)
       if np.any(mask):
          room=(1.0-h)*mask
          tot=room.sum()
          if tot>0:
             h += (diff/tot)*room
    elif diff<-1e-14:
       mask=h > (1e-15)
       if np.any(mask):
          tot=h[mask].sum()
          if tot>0:
             h[mask] += (diff/tot)*h[mask]
    np.clip(h,0.0,1.0,out=h)
    diff=target-h.sum()
    if abs(diff)>1e-15:
       mask=(h>1e-12)&(h<1.0-1e-12)
       idxs=np.where(mask)[0]
       if idxs.size>0:
          h[idxs[0]] += diff
          h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    return h
```
When diff>0, adding `(diff/tot)*room` to h: h+factor*(1-h). At mask h<1; if factor≤1? diff≤tot? target-s ≤ n-s? Since target=n/2, if s less, target-s ≤? room total = n-s, target-s≤n-s yes. factor≤1. Good.
When diff<0, factor=diff/tot negative magnitude ≤1? -diff/tot ≤1 if s-target≤s yes.
`h +=` with room array length n; for diff>0, room zeros for nonmask. Good.

`symmetrize_anti`:
```
def symmetrize_anti(h):
    h=np.asarray(h,dtype=float).copy()
    h=0.5*(h + 1.0 - h[::-1])
    np.clip(h,0.0,1.0,out=h)
    return project_feasible(h)
```
This returns symmetric-ish (project may break tiny; okay). For y mapping, use h from sym_full not this.

`sym_full(y,n)` expects len y n//2. If n odd? Use even. 
```
def sym_full(y,n):
    n2=n//2
    h=np.empty(n,dtype=float)
    y=np.asarray(y,dtype=float)
    h[:n2]=y
    h[n2:]=1.0 - y[::-1]
    # if n odd, middle? But n even. If odd, h[n2]=0.5
    if n%2==1:
       h[n2]=0.5
    return h
```
For even, good.

`grad_y_from_full(g,n)`:
```
n2=n//2
return g[:n2].copy() - g[n-1::-1]
```
If n odd middle? skip.

`default_h(n=500)`:
```
def default_h(n=500):
  if n%2: n+=1
  x=np.linspace(0,1,n//2)
  y=0.5 + 0.35*np.sin(2*np.pi*x) + 0.2*np.sin(4*np.pi*x)
  y=np.clip(y,0,1)
  h=sym_full(y,n)
  return project_feasible(h)
```

`resample`:
```
def resample(h,n_new):
  n_old=len(h)
  if n_new==n_old: return project_feasible(h.copy())
  x_old=np.linspace(0,2.0,n_old)
  x_new=np.linspace(0,2.0,n_new)
  return project_feasible(np.interp(x_new,x_old,np.asarray(h,dtype=float)))
```

Fast plan:
```
_FAST_CACHE={}
def get_fast_plan(n):
  plan=_FAST_CACHE.get(n)
  if plan is not None: return plan
  M=2*n-1
  Nc=1
  while Nc < 2*n-1: Nc <<=1
  Nw=1
  limit=3*n-2
  while Nw < limit: Nw <<=1
  d=np.arange(-n+1,n,dtype=int)
  q_idx=(-d)%Nc
  pos=d>=0
  neg=~pos
  lo=np.arange(n-1, -1, -1, dtype=int)  # n-1-j
  hi=np.arange(2*n-1, n-1, -1, dtype=int) # 2n-1-j
  plan=(Nc,Nw,d,q_idx,pos,neg,lo,hi)
  _FAST_CACHE[n]=plan
  return plan
```
Check hi for j=0:2n-1; j=n-1:n-1. np.arange(2n-1, n-1, -1) gives [2n-1,2n-2,...,n]. Wait length n? For n=2, arange(3,1,-1)=[3,2], but hi for j=0,1 should 3,2. Good. lo arange(n-1,-1,-1) for n=2 [1,0]. good.

`fast_c`:
```
def fast_c(h):
    n=len(h)
    Nc,Nw,d,q_idx,pos,neg,lo,hi=get_fast_plan(n)
    A=np.fft.rfft(h,N=Nc)
    r=np.fft.irfft(A*np.conj(A),N=Nc)
    Q=r[q_idx]
    csum=np.cumsum(h)
    P=np.empty(n+1); P[0]=0; P[1:]=csum
    total=P[n]
    L=np.empty(2*n-1,float)
    L[pos]=P[n-d[pos]]
    L[neg]=total-P[-d[neg]]
    c=L-Q
    # for numerical, set tiny negative? This can affect objective max if all negative? c should nonneg. Use np.maximum? For gradient selection, max okay. But if c negative tiny and m positive no. If max tiny negative due all? impossible. leave.
    return c
```
`fast_c5`:
```
def fast_c5(h):
    n=len(h); c=fast_c(h); return float(np.max(c)*(2.0/n)) if c.size else 0.0
```

`fast_grad`:
```
def fast_grad(h,w):
    n=len(h)
    Nc,Nw,d,q_idx,pos,neg,lo,hi=get_fast_plan(n)
    Fh=np.fft.rfft(h,N=Nw)
    FW=np.fft.rfft(w,N=Nw)
    conv=np.fft.irfft(Fh*FW,N=Nw)
    corr=np.fft.irfft(Fh*np.conj(FW),N=Nw)
    Sminus=conv[n-1:2*n-1]
    Splus=np.empty(n,float)
    start=Nw-n+1
    if n>1:
        Splus[:-1]=corr[start:Nw]
    Splus[-1]=corr[0]
    wcum=np.empty(2*n,float)
    wcum[0]=0.0
    # w may be length M; if start 2*n? For n=1 M=1, hi? okay.
    wcum[1:len(w)+1]=np.cumsum(w)
    S0=wcum[hi]-wcum[lo]
    return S0 - Splus - Sminus
```
Need ensure wcum length at least hi max 2n. M=2n-1, wcum length M+1=2n. Good. If w shorter? no.
`corr[start:Nw]` length? start=Nw-n+1, stop Nw, length n-1. Good. If n=1, start=Nw, slice empty, Splus[0]=corr[0]. But Sminus conv[0:1]. S0? hi [1],lo[0], wcum length2. okay.
Potential: irfft output length Nw; conv/ corr values beyond linear length may include wrap? Nw≥3n-2 so first 2n-1 conv and Splus negative range valid. Good.

`weights_cC`:
```
def weights_cC(c, alpha):
   m=c.max()
   sh=alpha*(c-m)
   shm=sh.max()
   e=np.exp(sh-shm)
   s=e.sum()
   if s<=0 or not np.isfinite(s):
       return np.full(c.shape,1.0/len(c))
   w=e/s
   if not np.isfinite(w).all():
       return np.full(c.shape,1.0/len(c))
   return w
```

`smooth_value_cC`:
```
def smooth_value_cC(c,alpha):
   m=float(np.max(c))
   sh=alpha*(c-m)
   shm=sh.max()
   s=np.sum(np.exp(sh-shm))
   if s<=0: return m
   return m + float(smx + np.log(s))/alpha? Wait sh already shifted? We did sh=alpha(c-m); shm=sh.max(); e=exp(sh-shm); logsum = shm + log(s). Return m + (shm+log(s))/alpha.
```
Implement.

`gradient_top`:
```
def gradient_top(h, active):
    n=len(h); dx=2.0/n; grad=np.zeros(n)
    # active np array indices in our d order
    for idx in active:
       k=int(idx)-(n-1)
       if k>=0:
          nv=n-k
          if nv>0:
             grad[0:nv] += (1.0 - h[k:n])*dx
             grad[k:n] += (-h[0:nv])*dx
       else:
          kk=-k; nv=n-kk
          if nv>0:
             grad[kk:n] += (1.0 - h[0:nv])*dx
             grad[0:nv] += (-h[kk:n])*dx
    return grad
```
For active length K, divide outside.

`line_search_h`:
```
def line_search_h(h, g, best_c, deadline):
    gnorm=np.linalg.norm(g)
    if gnorm < 1e-13: return h,best_c,False
    base=0.05
    # optional dynamic
    s = base
    for _ in range(14):
       if time.time()>deadline: break
       h_try=project_feasible(h - s*g)
       c_try=exact_c5(h_try)
       if c_try < best_c - 1e-14:
           return h_try,c_try,True
       s *= 0.5
       if s < 1e-7: break
    return h,best_c,False
```
Maybe use fixed steps better if dynamic base too small. Let's use fixed LINE_STEPS. Start 0.05 always. If direction good but step too large, shrink. Good.
```
for s in LINE_STEPS:
  ...
```
12 steps.
Could scale if gnorm very large: if gnorm>1, start smaller? g in C units, norm rarely >1. Use fixed.

`active_original_refine`:
```
def active_original_refine(h,best_h,best_c,deadline,phase_s):
    phase_end=min(deadline,time.time()+phase_s)
    h=project_feasible(h)
    h,best_h,best_c=consider(h,best_h,best_c)
    outer=0
    while outer<300 and time.time()<phase_end:
       outer+=1
       c=fast_c(h)
       improved=False
       for K in ACTIVE_KS:
          if time.time()>phase_end: break
          if len(c)<K: K=len(c)
          active=np.argsort(-c)[:K]
          grad=gradient_top(h,active)/float(K)
          g=grad - grad.mean()
          h_new,c_new,ok=line_search_h(h,g,best_c,phase_end)
          if ok:
             h_new,best_h,best_c=consider(h_new,best_h,best_c)
             h=h
             improved=True
             break
       # maybe try softmax if top fails? not here
       if not improved:
          break
    return best_h,best_c
```
`consider` returns best_h if h_new better. But if line search exact improved relative local best_c, consider will update. If due to best_h from other resolution lower, line search `best_c` passed is global best; h may be worse? Wait if best_h is from n=500 and best_c=0.38085. def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0=time.time()
    deadline=t0 + max(5.0, float(budget_s)) - 5.0
    best_h=None; best_c=float('inf')
    h0=None
    try:
       h0=np.array(initial_h_values,dtype=float).copy()
    except Exception:
       h0=None
    if h0 is not None and len(h0)>0:
       h0=project_feasible(h0)
       h0,best_h,best_c=consider(h0,best_h,best_c)
    else:
       h0=project_feasible(default_h(500))
       h0,best_h,best_c=consider(h0,best_h,best_c)
    if best_h is None:
       best_h=project_feasible(default_h(500)); best_c=exact_c5(best_h)
    if len(best_h)%2:
       hs=resample_sym(best_h, len(best_h)+1)
       hs,best_h,best_c=consider(hs,best_h,best_c)
       best_h=symmetrize_anti(best_h) maybe for sym
    # If best_c already very low? We need target. Could keep optimizing? If best_c<1/4? unlikely.
    # Phase 1 symmetric base
    if time.time() < deadline - 100:
       hsym=symmetrize_anti(best_h)
       hsym,best_h,best_c=consider(hsym,best_h,best_c)
       best_h,best_c=lbfgs_symmetric(hsym,best_h,best_c,deadline,120)
    if time.time() < deadline - 80:
       hsym=symmetrize_anti(best_h)
       best_h,best_c=active_symmetric_refine(hsym,best_h,best_c,deadline,80)
    # original base
    if time.time() < deadline - 150:
       best_h,best_c=lbfgs_original(best_h,best_h,best_c,deadline,120)
    if time.time() < deadline - 60:
       best_h,best_c=active_original_refine(best_h,best_h,best_c,deadline,60)
    # high res 1000
    if time.time() < deadline - 200:
       h1000=resample_sym(best_h,1000)
       h1000,best_h,best_c=consider(h1000,best_h,best_c)
       best_h,best_c=lbfgs_symmetric(h1000,best_h,best_c,deadline,150)
    if time.time() < deadline - 80:
       # maybe active on best and its 1000 sym
       h_high=symmetrize_anti(best_h) if len(best_h)%2==0 else resample_sym(best_h, len(best_h)+1)
       best_h,best_c=active_symmetric_refine(h_high,best_h,best_c,deadline,60)
       best_h,best_c=active_original_refine(best_h,best_h,best_c,deadline,40)
    # maybe original high res 1000
    if time.time() < deadline - 80 and len(best_h)!=1000:
       h1000=resample(best_h,1000)
       h1000,best_h,best_c=consider(h1000,best_h,best_c)
       best_h,best_c=lbfgs_original(h1000,best_h,best_c,deadline,80)
    # if time >200, n=2000 sym
    if time.time() < deadline - 150:
       h2000=resample_sym(best_h,2000)
       h2000,best_h,best_c=consider(h2000,best_h,best_c)
       best_h,best_c=lbfgs_symmetric(h2000,best_h,best_c,deadline,120)
       best_h,best_c=active_symmetric_refine(best_h if len(best_h)==2000 else symmetrize_anti(best_h),best_h,best_c,deadline,50)
    # final project
    best_h=project_feasible(best_h)
    best_c=exact_c5(best_h)
    return best_h, best_c, int(len(best_h))
```

def smooth_value_cC(c,alpha):
    m=float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(m): return m
    sh=alpha*(c-m)
    mx=sh.max()
    e=np.exp(sh-mx)
    s=sum
    return m + (mx + float(np.log(s)))/alpha
```

```python
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize

_FAST_CACHE = {}
_LINE_STEPS = (0.05, 0.02, 0.01, 0.005, 0.002, 0.001, 0.0005, 0.0002, 0.0001, 5.0e-5, 2.0e-5, 1.0e-5)
_ACTIVE_KS = (3, 5, 10, 20, 50)


def project_feasible(y):
    h = np.asarray(y, dtype=float).copy()
    if h.size == 0:
        return h
    np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0, out=h)
    n = len(h)
    target = 0.5 * n
    diff = target - h.sum()

    if diff > 1e-14:
        room = 1.0 - h
        room = np.where(room > 1e-15, room, 0.0)
        tot = room.sum()
        if tot > 0.0:
            h += (diff / tot) * room
    elif diff < -1e-14:
        base = np.where(h > 1e-15, h, 0.0)
        tot = base.sum()
        if tot > 0.0:
            h += (diff / tot) * base

    np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0, out=h)

    diff = target - h.sum()
    if abs(diff) > 1e-15:
        mid = (h > 1e-12) & (h < 1.0 - 1e-12)
        idx = np.where(mid)[0]
        if idx.size > 0:
            h[idx[0]] += diff
        else:
            h[0] += diff
        np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0, out=h)

    return h


def exact_c5(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    n = len(h)
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf")
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    val = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
    return float(np.max(val) * dx)


def consider_candidate(h, best_h, best_c):
    if h is None:
        return best_h, best_c
    h = project_feasible(h)
    if len(h) == 0:
        return best_h, best_c
    c = exact_c5(h)
    if c < best_c:
        return h, c
    return best_h, best_c


def symmetrize_anti(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    h = 0.5 * (h + 1.0 - h[::-1])
    np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0, out=h)
    return project_feasible(h)


def sym_full(y, n):
    n2 = n // 2
    h = np.empty(n, dtype=float)
    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=float)
    h[:n2] = y
    h[n2:] = 1.0 - y[::-1]
    if n % 2 == 1:
        h[n2] = 0.5
    return h


def full_to_y(h, n):
    return np.asarray(h, dtype=float)[:n // 2].copy()


def grad_y_from_full(g, n):
    n2 = n // 2
    return np.asarray(g, dtype=float)[:n2] - np.asarray(g, dtype=float)[n - 1::-1]


def default_h(n=500):
    if n % 2 != 0:
        n += 1
    n2 = n // 2
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n2)
    y = 0.5 + 0.30 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * x) + 0.18 * np.sin(4.0 * np.pi * x)
    y = np.clip(y, 0.0, 1.0)
    h = sym_full(y, n)
    return project_feasible(h)


def resample(h, n_new):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    n_old = len(h)
    if n_new == n_old:
        return project_feasible(h.copy())
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n_old)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n_new)
    return project_feasible(np.interp(x_new, x_old, h))


def resample_sym(h, n_new):
    if n_new % 2 != 0:
        n_new += 1
    return symmetrize_anti(resample(h, n_new))


def get_fast_plan(n):
    plan = _FAST_CACHE.get(n)
    if plan is not None:
        return plan

    m = 2 * n - 1

    nc = 1
    while nc < 2 * n - 1:
        nc <<= 1

    nw = 1
    while nw < 3 * n - 2:
        nw <<= 1

    d = np.arange(-n + 1, n, dtype=int)
    q_idx = (-d) % nc
    pos = d >= 0
    neg = ~pos

    lo = np.arange(n - 1, -1, -1, dtype=int)
    hi = np.arange(2 * n - 1, n - 1, -1, dtype=int)

    plan = (nc, nw, d, q_idx, pos, neg, lo, hi)
    _FAST_CACHE[n] = plan
    return plan


def fast_c(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    n = len(h)
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=float)
    nc, nw, d, q_idx, pos, neg, lo, hi = get_fast_plan(n)

    a = np.fft.rfft(h, n=nc)
    r = np.fft.irfft(a * np.conj(a), n=nc)
    q = r[q_idx]

    p = np.empty(n + 1, dtype=float)
    p[0] = 0.0
    p[1:] = np.cumsum(h)
    total = p[n]

    lin = np.empty(2 * n - 1, dtype=float)
    lin[pos] = p[n - d[pos]]
    lin[neg] = total - p[-d[neg]]

    return lin - q


def fast_c5(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    n = len(h)
    if n == 0:
        return 0.0
    c = fast_c(h)
    return float(np.max(c) * (2.0 / n))


def fast_grad(h, w):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=float)
    n = len(h)
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=float)
    nc, nw, d, q_idx, pos, neg, lo, hi = get_fast_plan(n)

    fh = np.fft.rfft(h, n=nw)
    fw = np.fft.rfft(w, n=nw)

    conv = np.fft.irfft(fh * fw, n=nw)
    corr = np.fft.irfft(fh * np.conj(fw), n=nw)

    s_minus = conv[n - 1:2 * n - 1]

    s_plus = np.empty(n, dtype=float)
    start = nw - n + 1
    if n > 1:
        s_plus[:-1] = corr[start:nw]
    s_plus[-1] = corr[0]

    wcum = np.empty(2 * n, dtype=float)
    wcum[0] = 0.0
    wcum[1:len(w) + 1] = np.cumsum(w)

    s0 = wcum[hi] - wcum[lo]
    return s0 - s_plus - s_minus


def weights_cC(c, alpha):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=float)
    if c.size == 0:
        return c
    m = float(np.max(c))
    sh = alpha * (c - m)
    mx = float(np.max(sh))
    e = np.exp(sh - mx)
    s = float(np.sum(e))
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.full(c.shape, 1.0 / c.size, dtype=float)
    w = e / s
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(w)):
        return np.full(c.shape, 1.0 / c.size, dtype=float)
    return w


def smooth_value_cC(c, alpha):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=float)
    if c.size == 0:
        return 0.0
    m = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return m
    sh = alpha * (c - m)
    mx = float(np.max(sh))
    e = np.exp(sh - mx)
    s = float(np.sum(e))
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return float(m)
    return float(m + (mx + np.log(s)) / alpha)


def gradient_top(h, active):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    n = len(h)
    dx = 2.0 / n
    grad = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)

    for idx in active:
        k = int(idx) - (n - 1)
        if k >= 0:
            nv = n - k
            if nv <= 0:
                continue
            grad[0:nv] += (1.0 - h[k:n]) * dx
            grad[k:n] += (-h[0:nv]) * dx
        else:
            kk = -k
            nv = n - kk
            if nv <= 0:
                continue
            grad[kk:n] += (1.0 - h[0:nv]) * dx
            grad[0:nv] += (-h[kk:n]) * dx

    return grad


def line_search_h(h, g, current_c, deadline):
    g = np.asarray(g, dtype=float)
    gnorm = float(np.linalg.norm(g))
    if not np.isfinite(gnorm) or gnorm < 1e-14:
        return h, current_c, False

    for s in _LINE_STEPS:
        if time.time() > deadline:
            break
        h_try = project_feasible(h - s * g)
        c_try = exact_c5(h_try)
        if c_try < current_c - 1e-14:
            return h_try, c_try, True
    return h, current_c, False


def line_search_sym(y, gy, n, current_c, deadline):
    gy = np.asarray(gy, dtype=float)
    gnorm = float(np.linalg.norm(gy))
    if not np.isfinite(gnorm) or gnorm < 1e-14:
        return None, y, current_c, False

    for s in _LINE_STEPS:
        if time.time() > deadline:
            break
        y_try = np.clip(y - s * gy, 0.0, 1.0)
        h_try = sym_full(y_try, n)
        h_try = symmetrize_anti(project_feasible(h_try))
        c_try = exact_c5(h_try)
        if c_try < current_c - 1e-14:
            return h_try, full_to_y(h_try, n), c_try, True
    return None, y, current_c, False


def orig_smooth_obj(x, n, alpha, mu):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
    dx = 2.0 / n
    c = fast_c(x) * dx
    val = smooth_value_cC(c, alpha)
    diff = x.sum() - 0.5 * n
    return float(val + mu * diff * diff)


def orig_smooth_grad(x, n, alpha, mu):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
    dx = 2.0 / n
    c = fast_c(x) * dx
    w = weights_cC(c, alpha)
    g = dx * fast_grad(x, w)
    diff = x.sum() - 0.5 * n
    return g + (2.0 * mu * diff) * np.ones(n, dtype=float)


def sym_smooth_obj(y, n, alpha):
    h = sym_full(y, n)
    dx = 2.0 / n
    c = fast_c(h) * dx
    return float(smooth_value_cC(c, alpha))


def sym_smooth_grad(y, n, alpha):
    h = sym_full(y, n)
    dx = 2.0 / n
    c = fast_c(h) * dx
    w = weights_cC(c, alpha)
    gfull = dx * fast_grad(h, w)
    return grad_y_from_full(gfull, n)


def lbfgs_original(h, best_h, best_c, deadline, phase_s):
    h = project_feasible(h)
    n = len(h)
    if n == 0:
        return best_h, best_c

    phase_end = min(deadline, time.time() + phase_s)
    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n

    if n <= 600:
        base_maxfun = 3500
    elif n <= 1200:
        base_maxfun = 2200
    else:
        base_maxfun = 1200

    configs = (
        (100.0, 1e-4, 0.9),
        (500.0, 1e-3, 1.0),
        (2000.0, 1e-2, 1.1),
        (10000.0, 0.05, 1.15),
        (50000.0, 0.2, 1.2),
    )

    x = h.copy()
    x_reset = h.copy()

    for alpha, mu, scale in configs:
        if time.time() > phase_end - 2.0:
            break
        maxfun = int(max(800, base_maxfun * scale))
        maxiter = int(max(800, 0.9 * maxfun))
        try:
            res = minimize(
                orig_smooth_obj,
                x,
                jac=orig_smooth_grad,
                args=(n, alpha, mu),
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                bounds=bounds,
                options={
                    "maxiter": maxiter,
                    "ftol": 1e-16,
                    "gtol": 1e-13,
                    "maxfun": maxfun,
                    "maxls": 40,
                },
            )
            x_new = project_feasible(res.x)
            c_new = exact_c5(x_new)
            x_new, best_h, best_c = consider_candidate(x_new, best_h, best_c)
            if np.all(np.isfinite(x_new)):
                if c_new < best_c + 0.01:
                    x = x_new
                    if c_new < best_c + 1e-12:
                        x_reset = x_new.copy()
                else:
                    x = x_reset.copy()
        except Exception:
            pass

    return best_h, best_c


def lbfgs_symmetric(h, best_h, best_c, deadline, phase_s):
    h = project_feasible(h)
    n = len(h)
    if n == 0 or n % 2 != 0:
        return best_h, best_c

    h = symmetrize_anti(h)
    h, best_h, best_c = consider_candidate(h, best_h, best_c)
    n2 = n // 2

    phase_end = min(deadline, time.time() + phase_s)
    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n2

    if n <= 600:
        base_maxfun = 3500
    elif n <= 1200:
        base_maxfun = 2400
    else:
        base_maxfun = 1200

    configs = (
        (100.0, 0.9),
        (500.0, 1.0),
        (2000.0, 1.1),
        (10000.0, 1.15),
        (50000.0, 1.2),
    )

    y = full_to_y(h, n)
    y_reset = y.copy()

    for alpha, scale in configs:
        if time.time() > phase_end - 2.0:
            break
        maxfun = int(max(800, base_maxfun * scale))
        maxiter = int(max(800, 0.9 * maxfun))
        try:
            res = minimize(
                sym_smooth_obj,
                y,
                jac=sym_smooth_grad,
                args=(n, alpha),
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                bounds=bounds,
                options={
                    "maxiter": maxiter,
                    "ftol": 1e-16,
                    "gtol": 1e-13,
                    "maxfun": maxfun,
                    "maxls": 40,
                },
            )
            y_new = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=float)
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(y_new)):
                continue
            y_new = np.clip(y_new, 0.0, 1.0)
            h_new = symmetrize_anti(project_feasible(sym_full(y_new, n)))
            c_new = exact_c5(h_new)
            h_new, best_h, best_c = consider_candidate(h_new, best_h, best_c)
            if c_new < best_c + 0.01:
                y = full_to_y(h_new, n)
                if c_new < best_c + 1e-12:
                    y_reset = y.copy()
            else:
                y = y_reset.copy()
        except Exception:
            pass

    return best_h, best_c


def active_symmetric_refine(h, best_h, best_c, deadline, phase_s):
    h = project_feasible(h)
    n = len(h)
    if n == 0 or n % 2 != 0:
        return best_h, best_c

    h = symmetrize_anti(h)
    h, best_h, best_c = consider_candidate(h, best_h, best_c)
    phase_end = min(deadline, time.time() + phase_s)

    y = full_to_y(h, n)
    current_c = exact_c5(h)

    for _ in range(350):
        if time.time() > phase_end:
            break
        h = sym_full(y, n)
        cv = fast_c(h)
        if cv.size == 0:
            break
        improved = False

        for k in _ACTIVE_KS:
            if time.time() > phase_end:
                break
            kk = min(k, cv.size)
            active = np.argsort(-cv)[:kk]
            gfull = gradient_top(h, active) / float(kk)
            gy = grad_y_from_full(gfull, n)

            h_new, y_new, c_new, ok = line_search_sym(y, gy, n, current_c, phase_end)
            if ok:
                h_new = project_feasible(h_new)
                h_new, best_h, best_c = consider_candidate(h_new, best_h, best_c)
                y = full_to_y(h_new, n)
                current_c = c_new
                improved = True
                break

        if not improved:
            break

    return best_h, best_c


def active_original_refine(h, best_h, best_c, deadline, phase_s):
    h = project_feasible(h)
    n = len(h)
    if n == 0:
        return best_h, best_c

    h, best_h, best_c = consider_candidate(h, best_h, best_c)
    phase_end = min(deadline, time.time() + phase_s)
    current_c = exact_c5(h)

    for _ in range(350):
        if time.time() > phase_end:
            break
        cv = fast_c(h)
        if cv.size == 0:
            break
        improved = False

        for k in _ACTIVE_KS:
            if time.time() > phase_end:
                break
            kk = min(k, cv.size)
            active = np.argsort(-cv)[:kk]
            g = gradient_top(h, active) / float(kk)
            g = g - np.mean(g)

            h_new, c_new, ok = line_search_h(h, g, current_c, phase_end)
            if ok:
                h_new = project_feasible(h_new)
                h_new, best_h, best_c = consider_candidate(h_new, best_h, best_c)
                current_c = c_new
                improved = True
                break

        if not improved:
            break

    return best_h, best_c


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    budget_s = max(5.0, float(budget_s))
    deadline = t0 + budget_s - 5.0

    best_h = None
    best_c = float("inf")

    try:
        h0 = np.asarray(initial_h_values, dtype=float).copy()
        if h0.size == 0:
            h0 = None
    except Exception:
        h0 = None

    if h0 is None:
        h0 = default_h(500)
    else:
        h0 = project_feasible(h0)

    h0, best_h, best_c = consider_candidate(h0, best_h, best_c)

    if best_h is None:
        best_h = default_h(500)
        best_h = project_feasible(best_h)
        best_c = exact_c5(best_h)

    if len(best_h) % 2 != 0:
        hfix = resample_sym(best_h, len(best_h) + 1)
        hfix, best_h, best_c = consider_candidate(hfix, best_h, best_c)

    if time.time() < deadline - 110.0:
        hs = symmetrize_anti(best_h)
        hs, best_h, best_c = consider_candidate(hs, best_h, best_c)
        best_h, best_c = lbfgs_symmetric(hs, best_h, best_c, deadline, 100.0)

    if time.time() < deadline - 90.0:
        hs = symmetrize_anti(best_h)
        best_h, best_c = active_symmetric_refine(hs, best_h, best_c, deadline, 80.0)

    if time.time() < deadline - 160.0:
        best_h, best_c = lbfgs_original(best_h, best_h, best_c, deadline, 130.0)

    if time.time() < deadline - 70.0:
        best_h, best_c = active_original_refine(best_h, best_h, best_c, deadline, 60.0)

    if time.time() < deadline - 200.0:
        h1000 = resample_sym(best_h, 1000)
        h1000, best_h, best_c = consider_candidate(h1000, best_h, best_c)
        best_h, best_c = lbfgs_symmetric(h1000, best_h, best_c, deadline, 150.0)

    if time.time() < deadline - 80.0:
        hsym = best_h if len(best_h) % 2 == 0 else resample_sym(best_h, len(best_h) + 1)
        hsym = symmetrize_anti(hsym)
        best_h, best_c = active_symmetric_refine(hsym, best_h, best_c, deadline, 60.0)

    if time.time() < deadline - 50.0:
        best_h, best_c = active_original_refine(best_h, best_h, best_c, deadline, 45.0)

    if time.time() < deadline - 130.0:
        h2000 = resample_sym(best_h, 2000)
        h2000, best_h, best_c = consider_candidate(h2000, best_h, best_c)
        best_h, best_c = lbfgs_symmetric(h2000, best_h, best_c, deadline, 100.0)

    if time.time() < deadline - 25.0:
        hsym = best_h if len(best_h) % 2 == 0 else resample_sym(best_h, len(best_h) + 1)
        hsym = symmetrize_anti(hsym)
        best_h, best_c = active_symmetric_refine(hsym, best_h, best_c, deadline, 20.0)

    best_h = project_feasible(best_h)
    best_c = exact_c5(best_h)

    return best_h, best_c, int(len(best_h))
```
